# THE ODYSSEY — LINEAGE JUKEBOX PROMPTBOOK

**Coverage:** 24 books · 152 scenes

This promptbook derives every cue from the atlas scene’s causal beats, named assets, and continuity handoff. The lineages are creative score genealogies, not historical claims.

## How to use

Use each book’s genome and AI-ECHO as the shared sonic constitution. Then run each scene lineage prompt as a cue-level mutation. Preserve the final handoff sound so the next scene inherits state rather than restarting.

## OUTPUT_B — BOOK SOUNDTRACK AI-ECHO

Build Book 1, “Athena Inspires the Prince,” from ritual recitation, palace chamber music, and civic speech: low aulos, muted lyre, frame drum, bronze bowls should feel inherited, handled, and repeatedly transformed rather than placed as costume. Start with “The Gods Consider Odysseus” in dry bronze ring, let the middle of the book pivot through “Mentes Questions the Prince” using slow heartbeat ostinato, and make “The Goddess Departs; the Prince Speaks” deliver the irreversible handoff into the next book. Keep the core motion at 68–84 with D dorian, A aeolian, modal suspension; free ritual breath gradually locking into an 82 BPM heartbeat. Mix it as voices and bronze centered; divine air high and wide; feast noise pushed far rear; use short stone chamber, tape halo, wing-flutter delay, low-pass feast bleed only when the drama changes state, not as constant decoration. The whole book should travel through grave, watchful, incipient, hopeful while recurring motifs retain their ancestry even under disguise, memory, storm, or violence. #ritual-chamber #bronze-council #awakening-prince #dorian #hidden-pulse

## SCENE LINEAGE PROMPTS

### OD-B01-S01 — The Gods Consider Odysseus

**Seed:** The Olympian gods assemble while Poseidon is…; Zeus recalls Aegisthus and argues that mortals…; Athena redirects the council toward Odysseus, captive…

**Inherited lineage:** ritual recitation, palace chamber music, and civic speech

**Local mutation:** Keep the divine layer above ordinary room tone: brighter, wider, and able to suspend or restart the pulse. Make the public body audible through claps, feet, vessels, benches, and call-and-response; crowd rhythm must change when authority shifts.

**AI-ECHO cue prompt**

Inherit Book 1's ritual recitation, palace chamber music, and civic speech—especially paper-dust hiss and slow heartbeat ostinato. Make the stage audible through Olympian council hall as the cue’s room tone, impulse response, and spatial bass boundary; Zeus as low thunder fifth and a single bronze strike; Athena as clear reed attack, bright bronze overtone, and decisive rising interval; Assembly of gods as a suspended choral field with missing and entering fundamentals; Poseidon's absence as a spectral transformation that changes harmony, depth, or clock behavior. Follow the scene’s causal clock exactly: The Olympian gods assemble while Poseidon is absent among the Ethiopians → Zeus recalls Aegisthus and argues that mortals enlarge the suffering they blame on gods → Athena redirects the council toward Odysseus, captive on Calypso's island → Zeus names Poseidon's anger as the obstacle and permits a plan for Odysseus's return. Keep the divine layer above ordinary room tone: brighter, wider, and able to suspend or restart the pulse. Make the public body audible through claps, feet, vessels, benches, and call-and-response; crowd rhythm must change when authority shifts. Work mainly at 69–83 BPM around modal suspension; use voices and bronze centered; divine air high and wide; feast noise pushed far rear. End by reducing the whole cue to the sonic consequence of “Zeus names Poseidon's anger as the obstacle and permits a plan for Odysseus's return,” ready to seed the next scene without a reset. #ritual-chamber #bronze-council #awakening-prince #dorian #hidden-pulse

**Continuity handoff:** The final beat—Zeus names Poseidon's anger as the obstacle and permits a plan for Odysseus's return.—must be serialized as the incoming continuity state for the next scene.

### OD-B01-S02 — Athena's Two-Part Plan

**Seed:** Athena proposes sending Hermes to Ogygia with…; She assigns herself the journey to Ithaca…; The gods accept the divided operation: release…

**Inherited lineage:** ritual recitation, palace chamber music, and civic speech

**Local mutation:** Keep the divine layer above ordinary room tone: brighter, wider, and able to suspend or restart the pulse. Build violence from readable cause-and-effect transients and tactical silences, not an undifferentiated wall of action music. Let recognition arrive through timbral memory before melody: a familiar resonance returns first, then the full motif.

**AI-ECHO cue prompt**

Inherit Book 1's ritual recitation, palace chamber music, and civic speech—especially wide divine air and feast-clatter polyrhythm. Make the stage audible through Athena as clear reed attack, bright bronze overtone, and decisive rising interval; Hermes as quick winged arpeggio and wand-click transient; Golden winged sandals as tactile close-miked percussion, never merely decorative; Athena's bronze spear as a tuned wood-and-metal transient with a long anticipatory resonance; Olympus-to-Ithaca descent as a spectral transformation that changes harmony, depth, or clock behavior. Follow the scene’s causal clock exactly: Athena proposes sending Hermes to Ogygia with the decree that Calypso release Odysseus → She assigns herself the journey to Ithaca to awaken courage in Telemachus → The gods accept the divided operation: release the father and activate the son → Athena fastens her golden sandals, takes her spear, and leaves Olympus. Keep the divine layer above ordinary room tone: brighter, wider, and able to suspend or restart the pulse. Build violence from readable cause-and-effect transients and tactical silences, not an undifferentiated wall of action music. Work mainly at 76–84 BPM around D dorian; use voices and bronze centered; divine air high and wide; feast noise pushed far rear. End by reducing the whole cue to the sonic consequence of “Athena fastens her golden sandals, takes her spear, and leaves Olympus,” ready to seed the next scene without a reset. #ritual-chamber #bronze-council #awakening-prince #dorian #hidden-pulse

**Continuity handoff:** The final beat—Athena fastens her golden sandals, takes her spear, and leaves Olympus.—must be serialized as the incoming continuity state for the next scene.

### OD-B01-S03 — The Stranger at the Threshold

**Seed:** Athena arrives at Odysseus's palace disguised as…; The suitors gamble and feast while servants…; Telemachus notices the unattended stranger and crosses…

**Inherited lineage:** ritual recitation, palace chamber music, and civic speech

**Local mutation:** Keep the divine layer above ordinary room tone: brighter, wider, and able to suspend or restart the pulse. Build violence from readable cause-and-effect transients and tactical silences, not an undifferentiated wall of action music. Make the public body audible through claps, feet, vessels, benches, and call-and-response; crowd rhythm must change when authority shifts.

**AI-ECHO cue prompt**

Inherit Book 1's ritual recitation, palace chamber music, and civic speech—especially dry bronze ring and free hexameter breath. Make the stage audible through Odysseus's palace threshold and hall as the cue’s room tone, impulse response, and spatial bass boundary; Athena as Mentes as masked reed motif with a hidden bright overtone; Telemachus as a narrow young pulse that gains range and rhythmic certainty; The suitors as overfed table percussion, crowd jeers, and unstable syncopation; Palace servants as a layered crowd rhythm whose density follows collective fear, consent, or dispersal. Follow the scene’s causal clock exactly: Athena arrives at Odysseus's palace disguised as Mentes and waits at the outer gate → The suitors gamble and feast while servants prepare wine, tables, and meat → Telemachus notices the unattended stranger and crosses the hall in shame → He greets her, takes her spear, and leads her away from the suitors' noise. Keep the divine layer above ordinary room tone: brighter, wider, and able to suspend or restart the pulse. Build violence from readable cause-and-effect transients and tactical silences, not an undifferentiated wall of action music. Work mainly at 68–80 BPM around A aeolian; use voices and bronze centered; divine air high and wide; feast noise pushed far rear. End by reducing the whole cue to the sonic consequence of “He greets her, takes her spear, and leads her away from the suitors' noise,” ready to seed the next scene without a reset. #ritual-chamber #bronze-council #awakening-prince #dorian #hidden-pulse

**Seed:** Odysseus's fleet sacks Ismarus and divides the…; He orders immediate departure, but the crews…; Cicones from inland arrive in organized force…

## OUTPUT_B — BOOK SOUNDTRACK AI-ECHO

Build Book 21, “Odysseus Strings His Bow,” from weapon rite, trial contest, secret alliance, and string revelation: monochord, frame drum, woodblocks, bronze key should feel inherited, handled, and repeatedly transformed rather than placed as costume. Start with “Penelope Retrieves the Bow” in waxed wood, let the middle of the book pivot through “Odysseus Recruits the Herdsmen” using failed strain accents, and make “The Bow Sings” deliver the irreversible handoff into the next book. Keep the core motion at 54–78 with A dorian, E pedal, pure fifth overtone field; a restrained 68 BPM contest pulse made of attempts, failures, and longer silences. Mix it as bow and breath hyper-close; crowd wide but suppressed; locks dry and exact; final string note fills the whole field; use wood resonance, strain pitch-bend, door-gate mute, long pure harmonic tail only when the drama changes state, not as constant decoration. The whole book should travel through formal, tense, humiliating, secretive, inevitable while recurring motifs retain their ancestry even under disguise, memory, storm, or violence. #bowstring #contest-pulse #sealed-doors #pure-fifth #inevitable

### OD-B21-S01 — Penelope Retrieves the Bow

**Seed:** Athena inspires Penelope to begin the contest…; The queen enters the storeroom, unlocks the…; She sits with the weapon across her…

**Inherited lineage:** weapon rite, trial contest, secret alliance, and string revelation

**Local mutation:** Keep the divine layer above ordinary room tone: brighter, wider, and able to suspend or restart the pulse. Build violence from readable cause-and-effect transients and tactical silences, not an undifferentiated wall of action music. Make the public body audible through claps, feet, vessels, benches, and call-and-response; crowd rhythm must change when authority shifts.

**AI-ECHO cue prompt**

Inherit Book 21's weapon rite, trial contest, secret alliance, and string revelation—especially held crowd breath and slow contest ostinato. Make the stage audible through Penelope as interlaced plucks and loom-like counterrhythm; Odysseus's bow as a tuned wood-and-metal transient with a long anticipatory resonance; Bow storeroom as the cue’s room tone, impulse response, and spatial bass boundary; Quiver and arrows as tactile close-miked percussion, never merely decorative; Twelve axe heads as a tuned wood-and-metal transient with a long anticipatory resonance. Follow the scene’s causal clock exactly: Athena inspires Penelope to begin the contest that will expose the suitors to slaughter → The queen enters the storeroom, unlocks the doors, and lifts Odysseus's bow and quiver down → She sits with the weapon across her knees and weeps before carrying it to the hall → Two maids bring the chest of axes and set the implements before the suitors. Keep the divine layer above ordinary room tone: brighter, wider, and able to suspend or restart the pulse.
